- **cs-agent-deployer**: Phase-4 specialist for making a Claude Managed Agent run without you. Turns a graded agent into a recurring POSIX-cron scheduled deployment (optionally self-grading each firing), an event-driven curl trigger, or confirmed on-demand use, then finalizes NEXT-DIRECTIONS. Invoke for phase=run-without-you. Uses deployment_builder.py, cron_validator.py, next_directions_writer.py.
